Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a homotopy algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 963. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 90/1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by homotopy generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 963, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 90. QED.
